# ui/utility/wzutility.lua

Project-added notes. See the [code](../../src/ui/utility/wzutility.lua).

The complete file retains lexical context. These summary excerpts do not approve the whole file or prove HMH invocation.

Original revision: `1a46c72b710c1820b63f72d22654f570286eee0b`. Original SHA-256: `2e54c9734f44a6684e009e196f19f7a7e653846f4fef80fe8b58b2d1515ca43e`.

Exact source spans, dependencies and bounded review evidence: [module record](../../provenance/modules/1c37efb71a2775af.json).

New notes and dependency decisions await independent review. Original build, native/VM, effective settings, service and gameplay limits remain open.

Selected source notes (excerpts; full records remain authoritative):

- `hmh07-lua-follow1:046` (original lines 3587-3602, 3570-3585, 685-687, 685-687): Initializes six controller progress/status models only when absent, then creates dynent/heal/revive/being-revived/bleedout progress and text/prompt children. Healing requires powerRatio>0, ability in-use and player not dead; Reviving uses reviveProgress>0, BeingRevived equality1, CowardsWayOut exact setting plus last-stand/not-ended/not-finished guards, Deposit both dynent progress and depositing>0. Ratio updates write the exact shader data; reviveProgress callback delegates smooth interpolation only for nonnil values. All status changes request state reevaluation. Close registration/postload precede SetupReviveProgressBar and SetupBleedoutProgressBar, then self return. This consumes combat state and does not implement revive/deposit timing or native cancellation.

- `hmh07-lua-follow4:034` (original lines 1916-1916, 2722-2722, 2918-2918, 1916-1916, 2722-2722, 2918-2918, 1916-1916, 2722-2722, 2918-2918): Returns the lower bound when value <= lower; otherwise the upper bound when upper <= value; otherwise the input value. A non-table intermediate is not guarded.

- `hmh07-lua:042` (original lines 681-683, 4796-4816): Builds the heal ability icon/meter/empty indicator and controller-dependent prompts. Model count/state/powerRatio choose Hidden, InUse, empty charging, charging or PowerBased; Deployed is explicitly false. Preference and visibility bits gate prompts; ordinary keyboard variants are false while WZ keyboard variant is enabled. Health stack count binds HUDItems separately. SetState/ammo updates may play Pulse, id changes may FadeIn when cycling inventory. Close is registered before tail PreLoadFunc and empty-health-flash property; PC hides the non-PC hold string. Original prompt alpha helper and unfrozen class bodies stay explicit source/native boundaries.

2643-2672, 2825-2839, 2841-2846, 2981-2992, 3062-3079, 3240-3254, 3435-3438, 4105-4112, 4715-4722, 4742-4746, 4790-4794, 219-225, 252-277, 400-403, 482-492, 707-732, 791-796, 813-826, 828-840, 858-865, 867-869, 979-986, 996-1002, 1923-1955, 2635-2641, 2643-2672, 2825-2839, 2841-2846, 2981-2992, 3062-3079, 3240-3254, 3435-3438, 4105-4112, 4715-4722, 4742-4746, 4790-4794): Compares numeric input against numeric Warzone maximum rank plus one. At/above threshold returns the special localized string; otherwise tostring(input). Missing numeric conversions are not guarded. Requires item model not dummy/collapsed. Reads selected slot and item id. If a slot-claiming pickup would replace a nonempty item, uses firstFreeSlot when not -1 or sends inventory_drop first when full. Then sends multi_item_pickup or multi_item_pickup_stowed_weapon with pickup id and chosen slot. These are clientscript requests; no authoritative inventory result is shown. Reads WarzoneSplitInventory.current and forwards controller/item/inventory_drop/amount to SendInventoryNotify. Errors if parent and child are identical. Stores parent&#91;field&#93; as a zero-argument closure; invocation calls child&#91;field&#93;() only if nonnil. It deliberately passes no implicit child receiver, and does not prove the dynamic field provider. Requires showPickupHint. Rejects INVENTORY_FULL, IGNORES_INVENTORY or AMMO_FULL flags. Uses forced-hint item index unless -1, then first free slot unless -1, then selected index or 0. Returns true only if that inventory item model equals the supplied widget model; otherwise false. Reads controller client number and both client team fields. Returns the literal short-circuit conjunction ending in team equality; missing team fields can return nil rather than Boolean false. Missing target platoon returns false. Otherwise returns false for platoonCount=0 or mismatched controller platoon; true for the remaining path. Controller clientModel:get() is not separately guarded. If same controller team, returns teammateIndex or 0 when falsy. Other teams return -1. Gets teammate index; -1 returns empty string, otherwise concatenates empty string with index+1 under Lua operator precedence. Uses TeamPlayerColorNameForClientNum to index ColorSet, then forwards that table to ConvertTableToRGB. Returns the second argument when the third equals 1; otherwise third times first. It does not inspect or move a death circle. Trial sessions localize the special tier-skip key; other sessions localize the supplied key. Reads old mapZoom. Only requested zoom <= MAP_ZOOM_MAX proceeds; there is no lower-bound check. Writes zoom first, derives normalized cursor position from engine bounds on KBM or markerPos otherwise, computes inverse-zoom pan delta, clamps pan to &#91;0,1-1/newZoom&#93;, writes pan and adjusts markerPos for clamp displacement. Division-by-zero and missing model/data values are not repaired. Requests the hashed engine pan operation with controller/x/y before setting the supplied model to {x=x,y=y}. Failure in the first request can prevent the model write. Captures Warzone pan/zoom and source horizontal layout, installs separate pan/zoom callbacks applying the exact horizontal transformation. A scoreboard-open subscription clears DisableZoomIn/Out then reasserts each when its trigger button flag is down. Closing the scoreboard has no reset path in this body. Captures Warzone pan/zoom and source vertical layout. Two subscriptions recompute the displayed row top/bottom positions for pan or zoom delivery using the exact retained expressions; it does not write map state. Computes sqrt(x*x+y*y), divides both supplied table coordinates by it and returns the same table. Zero-length inputs are not guarded. An existing _ignoreInputTimer returns false. Otherwise creates/attaches a timer and returns true; on invocation clears the timer field then sets the supplied model to the captured value. No explicit close call is present in the owned callback. Reads currentWeaponIndex. The recovered body calls the engine selector with 1 only within the current-index==0 path, then assigns a local 0 without calling it. Do not invent the missing reverse-selection call for index 1. With a weaponId and valid nonzero ammoType model value, returns the localized formatted text containing supplied label and ammo type. Otherwise localizes the supplied label alone. Reads hudItems.inventory.selectedItemName under the controller model and injects its get value into localization of the supplied string key. Checks the shared SplitterWidgetElement. If present returns whether its state is Open; absent returns false. The controller argument is unused. Reads objectiveN.unlockProgress from the supplied model. Nonnil/nonnegative progress is injected into the description key; absent/negative progress localizes that key alone. Reads weaponN.hasAttachments from WarzoneInventory and returns equality to numeric 1. Read f383_arg0.weaponId or0. In order, request HeaderWeapon:setText with Engine hash_4F9F1239CFD921FE("WZ/WEAPON_WITH_ID", tostring(id+1)), then HeaderWeaponEquipped:setText with the same provider and literal "WZ/WEAPON_EQUIPED", tostring(id+1). These are numbered-label text requests, with no direct weapon-name or ammunition lookup in this selected body. Localization, text rendering, input errors and provider effects remain unverified.

- `hmh07-lua-follow1:085` (original lines 628-656): Constructs kills, compass, WZ HUD menus, talker/VOIP and game-status children. Talker Visible requires spectator flag; full VOIP HudStart requires wzHardcore and not spectator, enemies-only HudStart AlwaysTrue and not wzHardcore. Status Right is AlwaysFalse and Left AlwaysTrue; filters split gameInformation/hudGameStat with exact HUD-left datasource. Parent HideForCodCaster and HideForSpawnSelect retain profile/show-model guards. scriptNotify dispatches create_prematch_timer or exact holding-pen hash with5000 helper duration. Close/postload precede custom WZ menu focus and quick-inventory auto-close setup5000, returns self. Source predicates and model consumers do not reconstruct VOIP network or match timer producers.

- `hmh07-lua-follow4:130` (original lines 4046-4046, 4046-4046, 4046-4046): Reads the supplied element model and forwards controller/model to FreeCursor.setupContext only when the model exists. Stores customGetHeight as an owned closure calling GetHeightByLocalTopBottom with the captured original element and later callback argument. Installation does not calculate a height. Gets mouse focus. If no menu was supplied but focus exists, uses focus:getMenu(). If a menu and detailed-view timer exist, closes the timer then clears its field. Uses a 1250-unit open timer. An already-open prompt cancels the menu close timer if present; a closed prompt creates an open timer when absent or resets it otherwise. On open-timer invocation it shows the prompt, marks open, closes and clears the open timer. Retain exact addElement and visibility requests in the trace; timer delivery itself is unresolved. Only an explicitly true open flag schedules closure. It creates a one-unit timer on the menu if absent, else resets that existing timer. On delivery clears the open flag, closes and clears the menu close-timer. This body does not invent cancellation of a still-pending open timer. Registers a ChatGlobal.MenuChatInActiveMode subscription whose closure hides the supplied widget on delivery without checking model value.

- `hmh07-lua-follow1:058` (original lines 4645-4674): Creates shared Director select screen with extra-home left/right filtered lists, featured playlists, mode list, safe-area/description and PC/Korea/tier-notification children. List callbacks refresh labels on lock/trial/lobby changes and descriptions on focus. Extra/mode trial-store branches containing AlwaysFalse are retained as inactive source predicates; unlocked/show-for-all/not-trial actions process the list and return true. Featured prioritizes trial store, unlocked action, missing-DLC overlay, ZM store, season-pass upsell and locked action. Mode locked action is PC-gated. Focus/lose-focus shows/hides tier notification. Safe-area menu_loaded sizes console and dispatches when unhandled. Korea event controls use exact active-event branches. State predicates distinguish live online demo/arena/default and LAN demo/arena/default. Close/postload precede pass-custom-function, UGC validation, WZ trial-video and PC redirect navigation setup. No effective playlist choice, store action, account state or native loader/runtime is asserted.

- `hmh07-main2:002` (original lines 3334-3345, 3334-3345): Test the block-local f0_local24(controller) predicate. - `hmh07-lua-follow1:170` (original lines 4796-4816, 4796-4816): Builds player-name, expanded-callout and squad-killed children and binds each to the parent model. HiddenPreference checks kill-identity hiding; Visible uses the full recorded demo/caster/HUD/death/killcam/UI/training gate. Settings and visibility subscriptions only validate state. Existing close and optional PostLoadFunc precede InitObituaryCallout and return self. Kill-event generation and callback delivery remain separate gaps.

Missing values can propagate nil rather than normalized false; no party-fill write or matchmaking operation occurs.

- `hmh07-lua-follow1:139` (original lines 4097-4099, 4097-4099, 4796-4816, 4796-4816): Builds a cylinder-mapped horizontal compass with 50 direction widgets, yaw-derived text and two native item layers using180. The yaw callback rounds/negates/adds360/modulos360 only for non-nil values. Separate PC inventory and preference predicates hide it. Preserve the Hidden predicate's recovered missing final return: its final local assignment is not silently returned. State watchers, input events, close and optional PostLoadFunc precede returning self.

- `hmh07-lua-follow4:202` (original lines 4849-4859, 4849-4859, 4830-4847): Creates equipment and FullConsumed grids plus prompt/backing/LED/frame children. Four generic keyboard states and FullConsumed disabled/unavailable predicates call AlwaysFalse, WZ keyboard predicate uses input type plus ShowQuickInventoryPC, Hidden2 returns true. FullConsumed filters type != ammo, then requests WarzoneInventory datasource. Registers exact model/input callbacks and class close, optionally PostLoadFunc, then calls SetQuickConsumeFilter before returning self. State priority/native datasource dispatch and actual consumption remain external.

- `hmh07-lua-follow1:097` (original lines 3334-3345, 3334-3345): Selected ui/main local bodies cover vehicle/reticle/lock-on replacement, common HUD hooks, spectator HUD, menu delivery and kill-stat updates only. Vehicle replacement may reuse prior type on force, skips same type or PC nonzero type unless forced, otherwise closes/resets filters/models before validating new type/moviecam/aerialcam; it resolves dynamic HUD factory, sets models, force-close ownership and menuOpened. Reticle replacement has same-type early exit and guarded dynamic class, wraps close to clear only same current object, then reevaluates visibility; lock-on always closes old, resets status/percent and uses defaultWidth for both axes literally. Common hooks always register common handlers and MP only when isZombie==false. SpawnSelectKillcam is multiplayer/non-demo, retaining REG4 receiver artifact; killcam HUD gets persistent force-close ownership. Spectator setup returns in demo, otherwise subscribes team once, creates WZ or generic CodCaster, initializes/focuses/one-shot postload and close unsubscription; non-spectator closes/clears it. In-game menu events queue before first snapshot, resolve xhash names through menu then overlay scans, reject duplicate pause/start menus/migration, update pause flags and ModelToUse, reuse startMenu or open menu/popup/overlay, subscribe close/state/animation and normally clear ModelToUse. PC WZ spectator quit early return can bypass final global clear; recovered gameScore reindexing remains literal. Last two selected callbacks write attacker/victim kills and guarded existing scoreboard killStats fields from unvalidated event data. Unselected registration/loader bodies and actual delivery stay open.

- `hmh07-lua-follow1:025` (original lines 681-683, 681-683): Constructs health/equipment/attachment pointer children, each Visible only when its respective available count>0; count subscriptions request reevaluation. Parent Open first requires quick inventory open, then the exact conjunction of HUD-visible/weapon-HUD-visible and absent demo/hardcore/guided/remote/hash/flash/scope/scoreboard/UI flags. All inventory/visibility subscriptions schedule state checks. Registers close, optional postload, returns self. Counts, state arbitration and inventory actions are external.

- `hmh07-lua-follow1:237` (original lines 4818-4828): ShowScoreboard forwards its sole argument; HideScoreboard forwards argument1, preserving the different signatures. Show/hide permission tests retain scoreboard-open/spawn-select, multiplayer/game-ended and WZ-map gates. SetScoreboardUIModels initializes local/controller0 fields, writes team summaries for multiplayer and conditionally player score, then writes all five column headers again unconditionally. Preserve duplicate header writes and absent native/model guards; no scoreboard or match was opened by this author.

- `hmh07-lua-follow4:269` (original lines 1959-1959, 2958-2958, 1959-1959, 2958-2958, 693-705, 4796-4816): Rejects vehicle, remote-control or view-only-linked players. Applies team-owner-specific objective flag gates unless the arg2/arg3/objective override holds; only then forwards objId and predicted client number to objectiveisplayerusing. Rejects demos and non-Warzone/nonfull inventory; otherwise tests ALSO_ALLOW_SWAP in HUDItems.weapon3dHintState, defaulting that value to0. Preserves the exact direct source forwarding contract: return CoD.WaypointUtility.ConvertToColorInternal( f110_local0 .. "_Base", f110_local0 ). Other ordered reads/writes below occur before this return; callee/native effects and missing-input error behavior remain external. Preserves the exact direct source forwarding contract: return CoD.WaypointUtility.ConvertToColorInternal( f111_local0 .. "_Muted", f111_local0 ). Other ordered reads/writes below occur before this return; callee/native effects and missing-input error behavior remain external. Preserves the exact direct source forwarding contract: return CoD.WaypointUtility.ConvertToColorInternal( f112_local0 .. "_Bright", f112_local0 ). Other ordered reads/writes below occur before this return; callee/native effects and missing-input error behavior remain external. Lazily maps meansofdeathlist bundle entries into enum-indexed rows, caches even an empty table and returns the table by identity. Compares g_gametype dvar with each ipairs entry of the vararg table; returns true on equal entry and false after iteration. The n field records vararg count but is not used by ipairs. Returns true for absent/falsy roundsplayed or numeric0; otherwise false. Defaults ratio1, divides ammoInClip get() or1 by clipMaxAmmo get() or1 only when both fields exist, then returns ratio,0,0,0. Numeric0 denominator remains truthy and is not guarded/clamped. Requests compass corners/north. Nonzero north-x swaps normalized x/y outputs; else nonzero north-y keeps them aligned. If both north components zero returns nil,nil. Does not guard corner denominators or clamp. Requests corners/north and interpolates world x/y with swapped relative coordinates for nonzero north-x, unswapped for nonzero north-y. Both zero yields nil,nil; no clamp is added. Clamps interpolation amount0..1 then requests component-wise RGB interpolation on the supplied receiver. Saves original clip_over handler then installs a wrapper. On delivered clip_over with no next clip and nondefault state, EnemyKilled decrements a numeric kill counter and either replays DefaultClip or sets DefaultState; other nondefault states reset. Calls originalClipOver last without a nil guard. Sets the nested prematch timer first. False show flag hides numbers and returns; otherwise if start time exists, >5s delay installs faction_start and a one-shot timer, <=5s plays Start immediately. Finally starts the countdown numbers clip. No explicit timer cancellation/close hook appears. Gets predicted client number, concatenates GetClientClantag then GetClientName, and returns it. No separator or nil guard is added. Truthy _allowingButtonRepeats returns without native call; otherwise marks true before the native push request. This is a flag, not a local reference count. Falsy flag returns; otherwise clears it before native pop request. Failure rollback is not provided. Lazily builds the set of written weapon names plus current gameMode suffix, then returns the slot selected by SafeGetModelValue(viewmodelWeaponName). The none/killstreak_remote table is defined but unused; cache is not rebuilt when gameMode changes here. Only existing hints table and truthy indexed entry return the localized entry. Missing paths assign hash0 then fall through without returning it. When abilityHintIndex model/value exists, returns hints-table and #hints==expected. Missing paths assign the none enum then fall through without an explicit return; retain GagdetHintIndex spelling. Returns when Warzone UI is hidden. Requires console, message and primary client; computes names/colors and localized message. Console path writes line fields with reversed-language ordering (missing secondary text is not cleared in the reversed branch), then forces subscriptions. PC path shows a formatted game event using secondary player when present, otherwise primary. No server callout generation or log persistence is proved.

- `hmh07-lua-follow1:110` (original lines 4963-4974): ShowOutcomeTransition rejects killcam/final/POTM/scoreboard flags before testing gameScore.currentState==outcome. Killcam requires allowkillcam1, non-splitscreen, not already played, team dead or game ended, then dead/spectating visibility. Spectate is true for elimination, or team_elimination after required AAR viewed. SetupGameEndScoreWidget initializes models/tables and subscribes scriptNotify: dispatches prekillcam/round/match/elimination/team-elimination setup, quick fade/POTM, clear, eliminator, teamRanking, HVO and matchBonus exactly as mapped below; notify payloads are not validated. Redeploy forwards one native call. ClearTransition emits quick_fade outside WZ, clears models, then WZ music/sound or MP start-menu notification. FirstTimeIntro sets location, plays intro once, then the elimination clip_over handler, only when postEliminationGameState is nonempty and no _postElimTimer exists, requests a post-elimination timer with interval argument1500, stores/adds it, and calls LUI.UIElement.clipOver in the same guarded arm. Empty state or an existing timer falls through without either action; actual timer delivery/provider behavior remains unverified; other branches delay in-game report. GetEliminatedString chooses exact hash by arg1==0. HasViewed returns true offline else stored aarViewed. Native/UI delivery and effective settings remain unproved.

- `hmh07-lua-follow4:139` (original lines 3177-3177, 3177-3177, 3177-3177): Returns a truthy engine getteamidbyxuid result. Multiplayer allies/axis use mpui keys; campaign allies/axis use the two exact hashed keys. Other cases return TeamName&#91;team&#93; or hash_0. No mode or team-data repair occurs. Returns CodCasterFactionAllies for a shoutcaster; otherwise the controller color-blind FriendlyBlue color. Returns CodCasterFactionAxis for a shoutcaster; otherwise the controller color-blind EnemyOrange color.

- `hmh07-lua:184` (original lines 3334-3334, 3334-3345, 3354-3354, 3354-3359, 3347-3347, 3347-3352, 685-685, 685-687, 677-677, 677-679, 681-681, 681-683, 3048-3048, 3048-3050, 3297-3297, 3297-3332, 3052-3052, 3052-3056, 3058-3058, 3058-3060, 2226-2226, 2226-2261): ui/main.lua1132..1165 builds common Hud unless existing/excluded gametype, then chooses WZ suffix for CoD.isWarzone OR gametype warzone (Campaign and CODFU/DOA overrides retained), and only when no T7HudMenuGameMode invokes Hud_WZ through dynamic factory unless non-WZ codcaster special case applies. Original effective flags/build remain conditional. Constructor initializes aarVisible/aarViewed only if unset, disabling automatic buttons only on PC, creates safe-area/ready/damage/chat/objective/results/scoreboard/hint/wheel/PC widgets. ReadyEvents scriptNotify dispatches hero_weapon_received/killstreak_received/levelup helpers; objective ShowNotification defaults are AlwaysFalse. MPHintText nonempty predicate and nonnil text link make this shared MP-named child usable on WZ. Sprays Visible requires held button plus no prematch-hide/last-stand/inventory/multi-pickup/remote/vehicle/demo/dead/round-end/scoreboard/spectator exclusions; Callouts similarly requires pressed+enabled and no theater/prematch/inventory/last-stand/dead/round-end/scoreboard/spectator exclusion. All corresponding model/input links reevaluate state, never produce gameplay input by themselves. Self states: HideAllButScoreboard=stage_ended; SpeedBoost=gadget_speed_burst plus gadget2 inuse; HideForCodCaster=caster with profile playerhud1. Circle/back hides scoreboard only through predicates and blocks the game event. Up opens quick inventory only with HUD weapon visibility, allowed inventory, no ended/demo/dead/scoreboard/spectating/UI/laststand/vehicle-hide/disable-setting; Down closes with its separate narrower gate. Weapon-next/prev/primary/secondary require Warzone, full inventory open and no demo. LB/RB/triggers and Cross prioritize health/equipment cycle actions when quick inventory open/profile cycle-items1/button-mapping agrees; they block the game event. Cross then has PC-gamepad deathcam fallback. PC-only spectate/deathcam/AAR/report branches retain exact additional conditions; if IsPC=false they do not execute, without excluding unconditional PC-looking child construction. CanUseQuickInventory notification closes quick inventory only if now disallowed and already open; character_unlock_update dispatches UpdateUnlockQuestState. menu_loaded processed, linked __onClose hook registered, captured f0_local0 postload invoked if present. PC final setup adds region checks/sizing; console final setup disables keyboard navigation and adds WZ armblade reticles; both setup grenade warnings/custom focus/cursor rules. Return self. Full exact callbacks/labels/branch ordering are in trace; unselected class clips/reset/close and external helper bodies are dependencies only, with no claim of original event delivery, network menu response or real execution.

Health/armor/portrait receive the predicted-client model when nonnil; heal receives playerGadget1. LowHealth requires health above zero and at most75. Armor repair lists accept REPAIR plus quickEquip1; health lists accept type health, unequipped0, quickEquip1. Links update the health inventory slots; constructor completion does not establish inventory actions or health mutation in gameplay.
